notes/theory/2026-10-13-intrusion-source-preservation-proof.md §§1,3,5, the immutable
birth-level induced-SCC correction in notes/design/2026-10-24-birth-level-scc-intrusion.md

supported original source program, lawful finite history and retained extrusion
occurrence, preserving original lexical/type/event scopes, complete Call,
correlated evidence and one shared source-admissible witness. No global semantic
FactId uniqueness, smaller source language or independent marginal witnesses.

Call, shallow Catch); research status retained. Operational/source safety opening

notes/design/2026-10-10-annotation-effect-hygiene-integration.md §1/§1.1: permission
is local subtraction authority, requiring matching actual inflow and selected
handling; allowance creates no contribution. The whole-proof closure guide §§1,3,4

## Construction, exact names and application

All delivered names have prefix YulangClosure.OccurrenceQualifiedConsumerR66.
sourceProbe/incidence compute the R62 owner/registration/recipe/component selection,
using R65.internalInputs: exact occurrence slot0 for route and slot2 for Lambda.
No old R55 routeFact/lambdaFact read authorizes a semantic fact. Incidence retains
OwnerReads, Identity and parameterRecipe. readRoot computes a selected current
physical slot, emission/admission/readback/relation, same artifact/raw entry,
qualified rootCandidates, rootSource/rootCause/rootTask and completed drain.
BeforeAncestry retains every R62 conjunct before ancestry; Root.checked is obtained
by readRoot, rather than used to select a root via ancestry.

source_incidence_checked, root_current_slot, root_source_fields and
selected_root_has_qualified_source_reads expose that exact R45 certificate,
R65.readAt witnesses/endpoints and R47 source/root evidence. rootQuery is built
from the same request use, probe fact, full permission.entry and selected root.
policy/readers use R61.native and its computed successful-call ancestry callback.
ancestry_reader_agreement proves the R65 update preserves the actual R47 ancestry
checker; complete_after_ancestry and first_entry_success_has_both_certificates
recover R47 Matches after that callback, retaining the outer physical identity
checks and every R61 schedule/resource/fuel prerequisite.

consumerInputs retains R57 arrival/Support/provider/path projections but replaces
arrival.current by that same R65.current. context constructs R49 internalInputs,
query, probe, certificate, current bundle, entry query, rootRelation and entry
probe from the very same Root. context_same_objects exposes these equalities,
including the identical full permission, fact and raw entry. ConsumerEvidence
lists the ten independent R49 remaining premises: permission family, unique
provider/entry/catch paths, checked entry/catch paths, Catch reads, terminal reads,
closed view and exact source arm. context_authenticates derives R49.Authenticated
and its boolean checker from those premises and ancestry. first_entry_applies_consumer
applies computed first-entry success to this constructed Context and derives
matching-target suppression, conditional only on that remaining consumer evidence.
It does not assume an independently mirrored Context or Authenticated conclusion.

current_bound_agreement and consumer_edge_reads_agreement prove physical reads
commute with the qualified update. qualified_local_decode constructs the R60
LocalDecode law by reuse of its actual physical decoder, rather than assuming
it. qualified_candidates_apply_consumer retains explicit outer successful Run,
Checked and terminal-equality premises and derives membership, R49.pathCheck and
R49.Arrival on this same qualified consumerInputs. No outer Run is manufactured
from local policy/Context success. successful_outer_run_contains_checked retains
the corresponding membership result separately. collected_catch_reader_retained
preserves the collected Catch slot51 reader exactly. R57 factOrigin/factPair are
retained only for the existing collected Catch evidence in the R49 arrival bundle;
semantic Internal and root reads use the R65 qualified adapters throughout.

Finite mathematical examples: finite_shared_fact_policy_and_consumer has two
source occurrence keys100/101 sharing semantic FactId(store2,index0), both readAt
successes, computed readRoot/first-entry success for selected occurrence100,
same fact/permission/raw-entry in Context, successful authentication and actual
R49.publish suppression of its selected provider terminal while forwarding a
separate contribution. finite_known_unmatched_preserves_occurrences authenticates
both identity owners, classifies knownUnmatched and forwards original ordered
[terminal,sibling,terminal] with duplicate terminal multiplicity retained.
finite_same_key_and_foreign_root_store_rejected rejects same-key route and Lambda
duplicates, foreign native store, substituted origin occurrence and an injected
foreign Context rootRelation (including failed matching publication). These are
finite mathematical inventories; no native execution, observed multiplicity,
source-qualified runtime counterexample or successful outer Run is claimed.

## Represented cases, remaining parent premise and ownership

Represented: all generic R57 branded source/Value/Effect endpoint constructors,
R65 semantic fact rows and complete provenance-edge multiplicity; R62 owner-local
Internal/first-entry checks; all R61 seven dependency constructors and retained
successful-call callback; exact R49 shared Context, Support/physical paths,
matching suppression and known-unmatched forwarding. R49's source-pattern model
retains its original first operation arm/empty-call syntax/wildcard continuation
fragment. Other original language forms are omitted by this finite model and
remain in the full parent obligation, not rejected from the source language.

First remaining premise/owner: Luna/source correspondence must interpret the
actual InventoryPackage into these exact Source/Inventory/Native projections,
including artifact/state/store/batch token and pointer authenticity, complete
provenance competitors, all selected native endpoints/TermViews, source arms and
path/provider inventory completeness. Actual R61 allocation/fuel outcomes and
outer successful Runs/Checked/terminal witnesses must come from the original
native execution. This conditional mathematics supplies no such execution.
The local source/native/production bridge, original A/B, full effect hygiene,
source safety, principality and termination remain OPEN. Independent review and
integration are pending with Luna; this receipt is not self-certification.

The ordinary skill checker was not run because it requires the Lake/cache/scratch/
build writes forbidden by the fixed packet. No project/default build is claimed.
Exact source declaration/proof closures were composed in memory under import Std;
unused inherited proofs were omitted, retained declarations came from frozen
source files, and comments were erased/inert in the check input. No project olean
was imported. Final memory-bounded groups cover all20 current theorem roots:
17 generic roots then3 finite roots, both exit0 without warnings, identical final
artifact hash. Every complete type and transitive #print axioms report is retained
below. Actual axioms are only propext and Quot.sound; no project axiom/sorryAx.
Compressed exact successful stdin plus direct source hashes permit reproduction.

Material failures are preserved in the chronological JSON below: the initial full
source check exceeded1GiB before reaching the new module; early closure slicing
left orphan documentation comments; one actual goal required the recursive
ancestry-reader equality; one fixture used the nonexistent EffectEndpoint.concrete
constructor, repaired to contribution; an expanded all-in-one audit again exceeded
the fixed limit and exposed a currentBound rewrite requiring explicit projection
unfolding. Final generic/finite groups repair those issues within the fixed limit.
An orchestration-only NameError(root_names) occurred before Lean and wrote no
artifact/checker result. Early attempt artifact hashes were sampled after subprocess
completion; only the final two groups capture the artifact snapshot before checking
and serve as final-byte acceptance receipts. Failed-attempt inferred sorryAx entries
are Lean's error placeholders and are not delivered proof acceptance.

No scratch/generated outputs or descendants. Checker totals/maxima and complete
version/source-scan receipts follow. Overall lane wall/model-token telemetry is
UNKNOWN; primary owns the actual2400-second envelope. All leased writes stop at
this frozen revision. Worker state STOPPED / WRITES STOPPED.
